+function readQianchuanAccountViewInPage(arg) {
+  const tag = arg && typeof arg.tag === 'string' ? arg.tag : '';
+  const wantClick = !!(arg && arg.click === true);
+  const visible = el => {
+    const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+    const style = getComputedStyle(el);
+    return rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0 && style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
+  };
+  const textOf = el => String((el && el.textContent) || '').trim();
+  const classHas = (el, name) => !!(el && el.classList && typeof el.classList.contains === 'function' && el.classList.
contains(name));
+  const insideForm = el => {
+    let cur = el && el.parentElement;
+    while (cur) {
+      if (String(cur.tagName || '').toUpperCase() === 'FORM') return true;
+      cur = cur.parentElement;
+    }
+    return false;
+  };
+  const shopView = /搴楅摵瑙嗗浘[锛?]/.test(document.body ? document.body.innerText : '');
+  const switchPresent = [...document.querySelectorAll('button')]
+    .some(el => textOf(el) === '鍒囪嚦璐︽埛瑙嗗浘');
+  const accountContent = [...document.querySelectorAll('[class*="summaryCard"]')]
+    .some(el => visible(el) && (el.textContent || '').includes('璐︽埛鏁翠綋娑堣€?));
+  const popup = { present: false, safe: false, reason: 'none', closeTag: null, clicked: false };

+  // 寮圭獥璇嗗埆蹇呴』鐙珛浜?shopView / switchPresent锛氶伄鎸″啀瑙傚療鏃讹紝娴眰涓嬬殑搴楅摵瑙嗗浘

+    const visibleCloses = closes.filter(visible);

= false;
+    {

+        if (!el || knownRootOrMaskVisible) return;

+          knownRootOrMaskVisible = true;
+          return;
+        }
+        if (visible(el) && classHas(el, 'mask')) {
+          let cur = el.parentElement;
+          while (cur) {
+            if (classHas(cur, 'overall-release-modal-v2-wrap')) {
+              knownRootOrMaskVisible = true;
+              return;
+            }
+            cur = cur.parentElement;
+          }
+        }
+        const kids = el.children ? [...el.children] : [];
+        for (const c of kids) walk(c);
+      };
+      if (document.body) walk(document.body);
+    }
+    popup.present = visibleCloses.length > 0 || dialogs.length > 0 || knownRootOrMaskVisible;
+    const block = reason => { popup.safe = false; popup.reason = reason; };
+    if (popup.present) {
+      if (closes.length !== 1) {
+        block('close-not-unique');
+      } else if (visibleCloses.length !== 1) {
+        block('close-not-visible-unique');
+      } else {
+        const close = closes[0];
+        const content = close.parentElement;
+        if (!content || !classHas(content, 'content') || !visible(content)) {
+          block('content-not-verified');
+        } else if (String(close.getAttribute('type') || '') !== 'button') {
+          // Must require explicit type=button (align chengfang v2).
+          block('close-type');
+        } else if (insideForm(close)) {
+          block('close-inside-form');
+        } else {
+          // Known root: ancestor must include overall-release-modal-v2-wrap.
+          let knownRoot = false;

+              cur = cur.parentElement;
+            }
+          }

+          // aria-label must be exactly CLOSE; empty never safe.
+          const ariaOk = closeLabel === '鍏抽棴';
+          let iconOk = false;
+          try {

+              ? Array.prototype.slice.call(close.querySelectorAll('iconpark-icon'))

+          } catch (_) { iconOk = false; }
+          if (!knownRoot) {
+            block('root-not-known');
+          } else if (!ariaOk) {
+            block('close-aria');
+          } else if (!iconOk) {
+            block('close-icon');
+          } else {
+            const controls = [...content.querySelectorAll('button, [role="button"], a[href], input, select, textarea')
];
+            const business = controls.filter(el => el !== close && el.tagName === 'BUTTON');
+            const links = controls.filter(el => el.tagName === 'A' && el.hasAttribute('href'));
+            const others = controls.filter(el => el !== close && el.tagName !== 'BUTTON' && !(el.tagName === 'A' && el
.hasAttribute('href')));
+            if (others.length > 0) {
+              block('unexpected-controls');
+            } else if (business.length !== 2) {
+              block('business-count');
+            } else if (!business.every(visible)) {
+              block('business-hidden');
+            } else if (links.length > 1) {
+              block('too-many-links');
+            } else if (business.filter(el => textOf(el) === '鎶曟斁涔樻柟').length !== 1) {
+              block('business-label');
+            } else if (business.filter(el => textOf(el) === '寮€鍚揣娆剧洿鎶?).length !== 1) {
+              block('business-label');
+            } else if (dialogs.length > 0 && dialogs.some(dialog => !dialog.contains(close))) {
+              block('dialog-not-owning-close');
+            } else {

+                const tt = String((c && c.textContent) || '');

+                block('danger-text');
+              } else if (!tag) {
+                block('close-tag-missing');
+              } else if (wantClick && close.getAttribute('data-qc-popup-close') !== tag) {
+                block('close-tag-mismatch');
+              } else {
+                try {
+                  close.setAttribute('data-qc-popup-close', tag);
+                  popup.safe = true;
+                  popup.reason = 'known-marketing-popup';
+                  popup.closeTag = tag;
+                  verifiedClose = close;
+                } catch (_) {
+                  block('close-tag-failed');
+
+                }
+              }
+            }
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }

彲鑳藉啀鎻掑叆锛?+  // 鍥犳涓嶅瓨鍦ㄣ€屽垽瀹氭椂鏄惀閿€寮圭獥銆佺偣鍑绘椂宸茶鏇挎崲銆嶇殑绐楀彛銆傜偣鍑诲悗绔嬪嵆绉婚櫎鏍囪銆?+  if (wantClick && popup.safe === true && veri
fiedClose) {

+      popup.safe = false;

+async function ensureQianchuanAccountView(target, shopCfg, opts = {}) {
+  const timeoutMs = sanitizePositiveMs(opts.readinessTimeoutMs, DEFAULT_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS);
+  const intervalMs = sanitizePositiveMs(opts.readinessIntervalMs, DEFAULT_READINESS_INTERVAL_MS);
+  const nowFn = opts.nowFn || (() => Date.now());
+  const sleep = opts.sleep || (ms => target.waitForTimeout(ms));
+  const deadline = nowFn() + timeoutMs;
+  const maxAttempts = Math.min(MAX_READINESS_ATTEMPTS, Math.ceil(timeoutMs / intervalMs) + 1);
+  const expectedName = String((shopCfg && (shopCfg.compassShopName || shopCfg.name)) || '').trim();
+  let switched = false;
+  let popupClosed = false;
+  let blockedSwitchUrl = null;
+  let reason = '鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘鍐呭鏈氨缁垨缁撴瀯鏈瘑鍒細鏈彇寰楄处鎴锋暟鎹?;
+  const fail = async message => {
+    const evidence = await collectLoginExpiryEvidence(target);
+    if (evidence.loginUrl || evidence.loginControlVisible || evidence.loginFormVisible || evidence.loginDialogVisible)
 {
+      throw new AuthError('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘璇诲彇閬囧埌鐧诲綍椤垫垨鐧诲綍鎺т欢锛氬皻鏈彇寰楄处鎴锋暟鎹?);
+    }
+    throw new DataGuardError(message);
+  };
+  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < maxAttempts; attempt += 1) {
+    const before = target.url();
+    if (!isQianchuanHomeUrl(before)) return fail('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘瀵艰埅鏈仠鐣欏湪鍙俊棣栭〉锛氭湭鍙栧緱璐︽埛鏁版嵁');
+    if (blockedSwitchUrl !== null && before !== blockedSwitchUrl) {
+      return fail('鍗冨窛鍙楅樆鍒囨崲鍐嶈瀵熸湡闂村彂鐢熷鑸細鍋滄鍚庣画鐐瑰嚮');
+    }
+    let view;
+    const closeTag = `qc-popup-${attempt}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
+    try {
+      view = await target.evaluate(readQianchuanAccountViewInPage, { tag: closeTag });
+    } catch (_) {
+      return fail('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘 DOM 璇诲彇寮傚父锛氭湭鎵ц鍏朵粬瀵艰埅鎴栨寜閽搷浣?);
+    }
+    if (!view || view.href !== before || target.url() !== before) {
+      return fail('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘鎺㈡祴鏈熼棿鍙戠敓瀵艰埅锛氭嫆缁濇贩鐢ㄤ笉鍚岄〉闈㈡暟鎹?);
+    }

+    if (evidence.loginUrl || evidence.loginControlVisible || evidence.loginFormVisible || evidence.loginDialogVisible)
 {
+      throw new AuthError('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘璇诲彇閬囧埌鐧诲綍椤垫垨鐧诲綍鎺т欢锛氬皻鏈彇寰楄处鎴锋暟鎹?);
+    }
+    if (target.url() !== before) return fail('鍗冨窛瑙嗗浘鏍搁獙鏈熼棿鍙戠敓瀵艰埅锛氭湭鍙栧緱璐︽埛鏁版嵁');

+        })();
+        entry.promise = p;

+      await p;
+      return;
+    }
+  }
   await browser.close().catch(() => {});
 }

+            if (typeof onSwitchClick === 'function') return onSwitchClick(calls);
+            return null;
+          },
+        };
+      }
+      // 鐧诲綍鎺т欢鎺㈤拡锛氶〉闈笉瀛樺湪鎵爜鐧诲綍鎸夐挳
+      return { count: async () => 0, isVisible: async () => false, click: async () => {} };
+    },
+    locator: (sel) => ({
+      first() { return this; },
+      nth() { return this; },
+      count: async () => (String(sel).includes('data-qc-popup-close') ? closeCount : 0),
+      click: async () => { calls.closeClicks += 1; },
+      textContent: async () => '',
+    }),
+  };
+  return { page, calls };
+}
+
+const VIEW_OPTS = {
+  readinessTimeoutMs: 500,
+  readinessIntervalMs: 10,
+  // 鍐荤粨鏃堕挓锛氭敹鏁涘彧闈?maxAttempts 闄愭锛岄伩鍏嶆祴璇曟満鎶栧姩瀵艰嚧鎻愬墠 break銆?+  nowFn: () => 0,
+  sleep: async () => {},
+};

+  assert.strictEqual(calls.closeClicks, 1, '鍞竴瀹夊叏寮圭獥鍙叧闂竴娆?);
+  assert.strictEqual(calls.switchClicks, 2, '棣栨琚伄鎸?+ 鍏抽棴鍚庢垚鍔熷垏鎹紙鍏?2 娆★級');
+  assert.strictEqual(calls.businessClicks, 0, '缁濅笉鐐瑰嚮涓氬姟鎸夐挳锛堟姇鏀句箻鏂?寮€鍚揣娆剧洿鎶曪級');

+test('ensureQianchuanAccountView锛氶伄鎸″悗涓嬩竴杞粛鏄笉瀹夊叏寮圭獥 鈫?DataGuardError 涓旈浂涓氬姟鐐瑰嚮', async () => {
+  const views = [

+// 鈹€鈹€ 寮圭獥瀹夊叏鍒ゅ畾锛氱湡瀹為〉闈㈠唴鎺㈤拡锛圴M 鎵ц鐪熷疄婧愮爜锛岄潪閲嶅啓鍓湰锛夆攢鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€鈹€
+
+class PopupNode {
+  constructor(tag, attrs = {}, text = '') {
+    this.tagName = String(tag).toUpperCase();
+    this.attrs = { ...attrs };
+    this.className = attrs.class || '';
+    this.children = [];
+    this.parentElement = null;
+    this.ownText = text;
+    this.visible = attrs.visible !== false;
+    this.clickCount = 0;
+    this.classList = { contains: c => String(this.className).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).includes(c) };
+  }
+  get textContent() { return this.ownText + this.children.map(c => c.textContent).join(''); }
+  get innerText() { return this.textContent; }
+  getAttribute(name) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(this.attrs, name) ? String(this.attrs[name]) : null
; }
+  hasAttribute(name) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(this.attrs, name); }
+  setAttribute(name, value) { this.attrs[name] = String(value); }
+  removeAttribute(name) { delete this.attrs[name]; }
+  click() { this.clickCount += 1; }
+  appendChild(child) { child.parentElement = this; this.children.push(child); return child; }
+  getBoundingClientRect() { return this.visible ? { width: 100, height: 40 } : { width: 0, height: 0 }; }
+  contains(node) { return node === this || this.children.some(c => c.contains(node)); }
+  querySelectorAll(sel) { return popupQueryAll(this, sel); }
+}
+
+function popupMatches(node, compound) {
+  const tag = /^([a-zA-Z][\w-]*)/.exec(compound);
+  if (tag && node.tagName !== tag[1].toUpperCase()) return false;
+  for (const c of compound.match(/\.([\w-]+)/g) || []) {
+    if (!node.classList.contains(c.slice(1))) return false;
+  }
+  const attrRe = /\[([\w-]+)(?:([*^$]?)=)"([^"]*)"\]/g;
+  let m;
+  while ((m = attrRe.exec(compound))) {
+    const actual = node.getAttribute(m[1]);
+    if (actual == null) return false;
+    if (m[2] === '=' && actual !== m[3]) return false;
+    if (m[2] === '*=' && !actual.includes(m[3])) return false;
+  }
+  return true;
+}
+
+function popupQueryAll(root, sel) {
+  const selectors = String(sel).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+  const out = [];
+  const walk = node => {
+    for (const child of node.children) {
+      if (selectors.some(s => popupMatches(child, s))) out.push(child);
+      walk(child);
+    }
+  };
+  walk(root);
+  return out;
+}
+
+/** 鏋勯€犲崈宸濊惀閿€寮圭獥/鏈煡瀵硅瘽妗嗙殑 mock DOM锛堢粨鏋勪笌鐪熷疄鎺㈤拡閫夋嫨鍣ㄤ竴鑷达級銆?*/
+function buildPopupDom(opts = {}) {
+  const body = new PopupNode('body');
+  const host = opts.inForm ? body.appendChild(new PopupNode('form')) : body;
+  // 榛樿宸茬煡鏍?overall-release-modal-v2-wrap锛沷pts.rootClass === false 琛ㄧず鏁呮剰鐪佺暐銆?+  let rootHost = host;
+  if (opts.rootClass !== false) {
+    const rootCls = opts.rootClass === undefined ? 'overall-release-modal-v2-wrap' : opts.rootClass;
+    rootHost = host.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', { class: rootCls }));
+  }
+  const wrap = rootHost.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', opts.dialog ? { role: 'dialog' } : {}));
+  const content = wrap.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', { class: 'content' },
+    opts.text === undefined ? '鏅鸿兘鎶曟斁 璁╁ソ鍐呭鏇存湁鐢熷懡鍔?浜嗚В璇︽儏' : opts.text));
+  const closes = [];
+  const closeCount = opts.closes === undefined ? 1 : opts.closes;
+  for (let i = 0; i < closeCount; i += 1) {
+    const closeAttrs = {
+      class: 'close-button',
+      visible: opts.closeVisible !== false,
+    };
+    if (opts.closeLabel !== null) closeAttrs['aria-label'] = opts.closeLabel === undefined ? '鍏抽棴' : opts.closeLabel;
+    if (opts.closeType !== null) closeAttrs.type = opts.closeType === undefined ? 'button' : opts.closeType;
+    const close = content.appendChild(new PopupNode('button', closeAttrs,
+      opts.closeText === undefined ? '脳' : opts.closeText));
+    if (opts.withIcon !== false) {
+      close.appendChild(new PopupNode('iconpark-icon', { name: 'oc-icon-close' }));
+    }
+    closes.push(close);
+  }
+  for (const label of (opts.business || ['鎶曟斁涔樻柟', '寮€鍚揣娆剧洿鎶?])) {
+    content.appendChild(new PopupNode('button', {}, label));
+  }
+  for (let i = 0; i < (opts.links || 0); i += 1) {
+    content.appendChild(new PopupNode('a', { href: '#' }, '浜嗚В璇︽儏'));
+  }
+  if (opts.extra) content.appendChild(new PopupNode(opts.extra.tag, opts.extra.attrs || {}, opts.extra.text || ''));
+  return { body, wrap, content, closes };
+}
+
+function runViewProbe(dom, arg) {
+  const ctx = vm.createContext({
+    document: {
+      body: dom.body,
+      querySelectorAll: sel => popupQueryAll(dom.body, sel),
+    },
+    location: { href: QC_ACCOUNT_URL },
+    getComputedStyle: () => ({ display: 'block', visibility: 'visible' }),
+  });
+  return vm.runInContext(`(${readQianchuanAccountViewInPage.toString()})(${JSON.stringify(arg || {})})`, ctx);
+}
+
+test('寮圭獥瀹夊叏鍒ゅ畾锛氬凡璇佽惀閿€寮圭獥锛堝敮涓€脳+涓や釜涓氬姟CTA+鑷冲1涓摼鎺ワ級鈫?safe 涓旀墦涓婃湰娆℃爣璁?, () => {
+  const dom = buildPopupDom({ dialog: true, links: 1 });
+  const r = runViewProbe(dom, { tag: 'qc-tag-1' });
+  assert.strictEqual(r.popup.present, true);
+  assert.strictEqual(r.popup.safe, true, r.popup.reason);
+  assert.strictEqual(r.popup.closeTag, 'qc-tag-1');

1:async function ensureQianchuanAccountView(target, shopCfg, opts = {}) {
2:  const timeoutMs = sanitizePositiveMs(opts.readinessTimeoutMs, DEFAULT_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS);
3:  const intervalMs = sanitizePositiveMs(opts.readinessIntervalMs, DEFAULT_READINESS_INTERVAL_MS);
4:  const nowFn = opts.nowFn || (() => Date.now());
5:  const sleep = opts.sleep || (ms => target.waitForTimeout(ms));
6:  const deadline = nowFn() + timeoutMs;
7:  const maxAttempts = Math.min(MAX_READINESS_ATTEMPTS, Math.ceil(timeoutMs / intervalMs) + 1);
8:  const expectedName = String((shopCfg && (shopCfg.compassShopName || shopCfg.name)) || '').trim();
9:  let switched = false;

52:class PopupNode {
53:  constructor(tag, attrs = {}, text = '') {
54:    this.tagName = String(tag).toUpperCase();
55:    this.attrs = { ...attrs };
56:    this.className = attrs.class || '';
57:    this.children = [];
58:    this.parentElement = null;
59:    this.ownText = text;
60:    this.visible = attrs.visible !== false;
61:    this.clickCount = 0;
62:    this.classList = { contains: c => String(this.className).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).includes(c) };
63:  }
64:  get textContent() { return this.ownText + this.children.map(c => c.textContent).join(''); }
65:  get innerText() { return this.textContent; }
66:  getAttribute(name) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(this.attrs, name) ? String(this.attrs[name]) : nu
ll; }
67:  hasAttribute(name) { return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(this.attrs, name); }
68:  setAttribute(name, value) { this.attrs[name] = String(value); }
69:  removeAttribute(name) { delete this.attrs[name]; }
70:  click() { this.clickCount += 1; }
71:  appendChild(child) { child.parentElement = this; this.children.push(child); return child; }
72:  getBoundingClientRect() { return this.visible ? { width: 100, height: 40 } : { width: 0, height: 0 }; }
73:  contains(node) { return node === this || this.children.some(c => c.contains(node)); }
74:  querySelectorAll(sel) { return popupQueryAll(this, sel); }
75:}
76:
77:function popupMatches(node, compound) {
78:  const tag = /^([a-zA-Z][\w-]*)/.exec(compound);
79:  if (tag && node.tagName !== tag[1].toUpperCase()) return false;
80:  for (const c of compound.match(/\.([\w-]+)/g) || []) {
81:    if (!node.classList.contains(c.slice(1))) return false;
82:  }
83:  const attrRe = /\[([\w-]+)(?:([*^$]?)=)"([^"]*)"\]/g;
84:  let m;
85:  while ((m = attrRe.exec(compound))) {
86:    const actual = node.getAttribute(m[1]);
87:    if (actual == null) return false;
88:    if (m[2] === '=' && actual !== m[3]) return false;
89:    if (m[2] === '*=' && !actual.includes(m[3])) return false;
90:  }
91:  return true;
92:}
93:
94:function popupQueryAll(root, sel) {
95:  const selectors = String(sel).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
96:  const out = [];
97:  const walk = node => {
98:    for (const child of node.children) {
99:      if (selectors.some(s => popupMatches(child, s))) out.push(child);
100:      walk(child);
101:    }
102:  };
103:  walk(root);
104:  return out;
105:}
106:
107:/** 鏋勯€犲崈宸濊惀閿€寮圭獥/鏈煡瀵硅瘽妗嗙殑 mock DOM锛堢粨鏋勪笌鐪熷疄鎺㈤拡閫夋嫨鍣ㄤ竴鑷达級銆?*/
108:function buildPopupDom(opts = {}) {
109:  const body = new PopupNode('body');
110:  const host = opts.inForm ? body.appendChild(new PopupNode('form')) : body;
111:  // 榛樿宸茬煡鏍?overall-release-modal-v2-wrap锛沷pts.rootClass === false 琛ㄧず鏁呮剰鐪佺暐銆?
112:  let rootHost = host;
113:  if (opts.rootClass !== false) {
114:    const rootCls = opts.rootClass === undefined ? 'overall-release-modal-v2-wrap' : opts.rootClass;
115:    rootHost = host.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', { class: rootCls }));
116:  }
117:  const wrap = rootHost.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', opts.dialog ? { role: 'dialog' } : {}));
118:  const content = wrap.appendChild(new PopupNode('div', { class: 'content' },
119:    opts.text === undefined ? '鏅鸿兘鎶曟斁 璁╁ソ鍐呭鏇存湁鐢熷懡鍔?浜嗚В璇︽儏' : opts.text));
120:  const closes = [];
121:  const closeCount = opts.closes === undefined ? 1 : opts.closes;
122:  for (let i = 0; i < closeCount; i += 1) {
123:    const closeAttrs = {
124:      class: 'close-button',
125:      visible: opts.closeVisible !== false,
126:    };
127:    if (opts.closeLabel !== null) closeAttrs['aria-label'] = opts.closeLabel === undefined ? '鍏抽棴' : opts.closeLabe
l;
128:    if (opts.closeType !== null) closeAttrs.type = opts.closeType === undefined ? 'button' : opts.closeType;
129:    const close = content.appendChild(new PopupNode('button', closeAttrs,
130:      opts.closeText === undefined ? '脳' : opts.closeText));
131:    if (opts.withIcon !== false) {
132:      close.appendChild(new PopupNode('iconpark-icon', { name: 'oc-icon-close' }));
133:    }
134:    closes.push(close);
135:  }
136:  for (const label of (opts.business || ['鎶曟斁涔樻柟', '寮€鍚揣娆剧洿鎶?])) {
137:    content.appendChild(new PopupNode('button', {}, label));
138:  }
139:  for (let i = 0; i < (opts.links || 0); i += 1) {
140:    content.appendChild(new PopupNode('a', { href: '#' }, '浜嗚В璇︽儏'));
141:  }
142:  if (opts.extra) content.appendChild(new PopupNode(opts.extra.tag, opts.extra.attrs || {}, opts.extra.text || ''))
;
143:  return { body, wrap, content, closes };
144:}
145:
146:function runViewProbe(dom, arg) {
147:  const ctx = vm.createContext({
148:    document: {
149:      body: dom.body,
150:      querySelectorAll: sel => popupQueryAll(dom.body, sel),
151:    },
152:    location: { href: QC_ACCOUNT_URL },
153:    getComputedStyle: () => ({ display: 'block', visibility: 'visible' }),
154:  });
155:  return vm.runInContext(`(${readQianchuanAccountViewInPage.toString()})(${JSON.stringify(arg || {})})`, ctx);
156:}
